- Revision-8 fresh-shell and legacy-commit integration: open/append/readmit/close execute in distinct shells with one live harness PID and unchanged owner; missing/malformed/dead current identity and live-foreign/malformed recorded owner retain their precedence without mutation; stale takeover occurs only after candidate PASS; prepare/standalone-commit/cleanup execute as three tool calls through success, halt, index drift, hook denial, Git failure, interruption, and cleanup failure; the former bundled shape retains the pinned public denial; cleanup actions are independent and candidate-only; a successful commit is never retried; FR-221 marker and chain authorization paths remain independently accepted; workflow/commit prose, worktree-merge's composite lock epoch, hook integration, and repository conformance remain synchronized

- Phase-3/4 integration uses a real bare remote and real linked worktrees to exercise a clean merge; control approval; two concurrent disjoint merges plus two standalone-push contenders with byte-stable singleton serialization; atomic same-worktree refusal; locked start/refresh fetch failure and recovery; remote-only carry-forward, initial destination absence, pre-invocation authoritative deletion, and deletion after the final read; conflict-free HEAD rewrite; real rebase conflicts/continuation/abort; standalone successful-no-op plus nonce/reflog crash recovery and attribution refusal; two distinct attempted heads with only the historical head contained followed by both a distinct fresh intent and an identity-distinct repeated-OID no-op intent; observation-only remote movement distinct from actual `[rejected] (non-fast-forward)` and `[rejected] (fetch first)` outcomes; remote churn at the eighth epoch; integrated gate failure; post-push crash that blocks commit finalize except for the exact activation-repair tuple, inner/outer release recovery, and proven-not-landed inactive abort; cleanup retry; a simulated macOS entrant and Linux entrant contending on the same mandatory portable owner while Linux also holds secondary `flock`; outer-only/empty-dir/complete owner topologies; fallback-owner-only reservation; crashed-reservation, foreign-owner, and unprovable-fence operator-quarantine boundaries; and provisional-recovery-reservation crash/reclaim/release; two-contender stale per-chain lease recovery; safe live/foreign lease handling; and a survivor fence that excludes every later merge/push without merge commits or force operations
- V2 hook/activation integration runs the complete matcher corpus for `forge commit finalize`, `forge merge finalize`, and `forge push`; legacy/enabled/malformed manifests; committed-versus-working-tree mode; merge-approval operator denial; allowed CLI plus appended raw Git; every quote/interpreter/path form; exact deny JSON/literals; invalid-mode repair through a candidate-bound finalize; and byte-identical phase-1 behavior while legacy is active
- V2 corpus/manifest integration consumes rather than copies all v1 artifacts, verifies the three recorded v1 hashes, the exact seven-file DM-016 inventory, schema identifiers, complete sorted union, and transition/recovery/push/cleanup/activated-denial/last-line results bound by the manifest
- Future phase-5 admission integration holds operator-provided halt plus common/commit/all-chain locks across two real-filesystem snapshots, blocks every live or malformed DM-012 chain and every valid-unexpired or ambiguous marker, detects an installed-surface inventory omission and lineage gap, and cannot delete a marker/parser/test or clear the halt under Revision-7 authority

- Revision-9 integration matrix: run the two real legacy journals through binding-aware rendering; run typed builders and batch recovery across separate reader/writer processes; drive both a commit and merge chain through paired run/task binding, event-carried journal drain, crash/replay, explicit terminal task update, ordinary journal-only close validation, and deterministic archive mode; ingest an already-landed chain into an open fixture run and prove identical replay; render phase 0 only in a temporary/absent destination with a fixture operator decision and prove the existing coordination archive hash unchanged. Focused tests fail when each binding, reader lock, intent/suffix/receipt, outbox-order, ingest-proof, rerender, size, or legacy-approval control is disabled in memory.
- Revision-11 coordination integration: replay landed commit families through historical receipts and the landing window; enumerate a healthy chain beside unreadable unrelated state; seal and consume exact captured/absent tombstones through crash recovery; ingest historical evidence without a manual mirror; bind, transition, and ingest committed changelog outputs while refusing arbitrary caller designations; execute keyed readmission through journal durability and registry publication; and recover one production-shaped missing readmission receipt before a later append while every unsafe gap and unpublished shrink remains byte-preserving and pending.
- Revision-11 gate integration: a late fenced Gate-2 operation receives and exhausts only its own bounded publication budget, while archive mode with a configured changelog proceeds without scheduling it and direct archive changelog execution retains the exact mutating-gate refusal.

### E2E Smoke

- Full run on a fixture repo with `fake_codex.py`: two-commit init bootstrap/activation → workflow (implementer + review-cheap + review-final gates) → `validate --gates → run_closed → validate --gates → archive → report.md`; then the same journal validates clean under upstream-shape `validate`
- Revision-8 coordination smoke: a sparse legacy journal remains readable, all seven strict minimum new records append, an A→B→C successor chain carries and then releases its ancestral reservation, an inert empty placeholder does not wedge unrelated coordination, a nonempty orphan names its path, and distinct shells retain one live session identity without changing any refusal-path bytes
- Revision-8 legacy commit smoke: marker creation follows live-identity validation, prepare acquires and rehashes under the persistent lock, the next Bash call is only the safely quoted commit segment, and unconditional cleanup releases the lock and deletes only the candidate marker across success/failure/interruption without changing FR-090 or FR-221 acceptance bytes
- Verification-expansion smoke on a fixture repo: assertion sensor + invariant gate → fast docs commit with independent guard recomputation → standard promotion → advisory mutation merge → mechanical/semantic drift report → durable archive/report ordering
- Opt-in live smoke (real Codex CLI): one `codex exec` launch per distinct configured model (`gpt-5.6-sol`) verifying model acceptance and the effort config key at each configured effort
- Phase-3 smoke: registered worktree → merge start → Gate 1/advisory mutation/Gate 2 → unconditional review-final → control approval when applicable → bounded locked rebase/final mechanical checks → exact ordinary non-force push → durable pushed truth → non-force cleanup
- Phase-4 close smoke: `bd dolt push` → `python3 "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/scripts/forge/cli.py" push` → `git status`, with short-circuit failure; activated raw commit/push denial while all three sanctioned CLI producer paths remain legal; legal no-provenance default-branch push; and invalid-mode repair
- Every required serial suite record is judged from that suite's own output tail, never a background wrapper's exit code; after the last defect fix, applicable end-to-end checks follow FR-121 and full unittest discovery passes twice consecutively before phase-5 admission can be claimed

---

- Revision-9 smoke: activated `run-open` → run/task-bound commit and merge events → receipted typed journal records → explicit task finish → journal-only `validate --gates` → bounded exact chain-evidence archive → rerender-equality archive-only commit → report, with one injected outbox crash recovered before the next transition and upstream seven-type/plain-validation parity retained.
- Revision-9 legacy smoke: the committed 109-record GH#5 fixture validates under its declaration while an equivalent new append refuses; the separately closed phase-0 fixture renders only under a per-instance operator decision naming its recovered HEAD, creates only the absent archive, and leaves every prior DM-008 archive and source journal byte-identical.

## 12. Success Criteria

- **SC-001**: `python3 -m unittest discover -s tests` exits 0 from the plugin root on macOS and Linux with bare Python ≥ 3.10 (no packages installed).
- **SC-002**: `validate` without `--gates` on the extended replay journal produces a payload with exactly the four upstream keys and exit 0; with `--gates`, the same journal yields `ok: true`, `profile: "gates"`, exit 0.
- **SC-003**: The two gates-negative fixtures produce exactly the FR-021 and FR-022 issue strings respectively, with exit 1.
- **SC-004**: On a freshly initialized scaffold repo before region filling, `grep -rln "forge-init:" forge-project.md` is non-empty and the Gate 1 command exits 1; after `/forge:init` completes, the grep is empty and Gate 1 exits 0 on the clean tree.
- **SC-005**: When `HEAD:.forge-manifest` contains anchored `^plugin_ref: ` or a worktree manifest exists that does not parse as upstream schema, absence of `.forge/tmp/authorized/<staged-diff-sha256>` makes the guard deny `git commit` — including `git -C <path> commit` and `cd x && git commit`; a fresh matching marker for the invoking Git context allows, while an upstream-only manifest remains halt-only and a staged deletion of a committed plugin manifest stays armed. With `AGENT_HALT` present it denies both `git commit` and `git push` in any repo.
- **SC-006**: `codex execpolicy check --rules .codex/rules/forge.rules` reports decision `forbidden` for `git push --force`, `git push origin HEAD`, `git reset --hard`, `git clean -fd`, and `rm -rf /`.
- **SC-007**: `run-evals.sh` on an empty task dir exits 2; on the three seeded fixtures with matching baselines exits 0; flipping one baseline exits 1.
- **SC-008**: `grep -ri opencode` over the plugin tree returns matches only under `UPSTREAM`, `docs/design/`, `docs/specs/`, in `scripts/forge/migrate-upstream.py`, and in tests that exercise that migration reader; it returns no match in any other shipped file. The exception permits migration-time reading required by FR-181, not legacy runtime support.
- **SC-009**: The E2E smoke run (fixture repo, `fake_codex.py`) completes twice consecutively with `run_closed: passed` and gate verifications present, per FR-121's two-consecutive-runs rule applied to the release itself.
- **SC-010**: A working-tree edit that replaces a committed invariant or tier policy cannot change any hook/gate command or authorize a fast commit; invariant timeouts kill the full process group and cap combined output at 65,536 bytes.
- **SC-011**: A docs-only fast commit completes without Step 4 review only when the classifier and guard independently derive fast from the exact staged diff and the same committed `risk-tiers`/`trigger-paths`/`file-categories` source; Python de-indent, YAML nesting, dependency-block matches, and unknown-stack manifests derive at least standard, and policy/eligibility drift produces the exact FR-154 denial.
- **SC-012**: `drift-check.sh` emits deterministic, canonical eight-key Drift summary schema v1 JSON for exit 0, exit 1, dirty-precondition exit 2, and non-dirty exit 2, including the exact available/unavailable `journal_patterns` carriage, all thirteen counters, and event-prune outcome when telemetry is available; repeated same-date runs overwrite only the transient JSON while `/forge:drift` consumes only that JSON and commits one collision-free append-only report whose counters remain unchanged if a Stop hook appends to `.forge/tmp/telemetry.csv`, and only a CRITICAL result writes the operator-cleared run-opening block.
- **SC-013**: `/forge:report` refuses before a clean archive exists in HEAD and succeeds after the archive commit, with the original goal, task acceptance, decisions/bases, plan documents, gate evidence, risks, and command-derived SHAs preserved.
- **SC-014**: Init succeeds with dcg absent, invokes the exact allow command once when needed, skips it on re-init, fills all fourteen regions including all five Revision 2 additions, creates all DM-007/DM-008 layouts including candidates/migrations/gotchas and content-addressed authorization state, and remains dependency-free on macOS and Linux.
- **SC-015**: A touched unwaived Python assertion-free test blocks, a non-Python heuristic finding and an absent seeded heuristic remain advisory, and a valid `# forge-assertion-waiver: <reason>` suppresses only its containing file while remaining visible in review evidence; the resulting blocking/advisory/waived dispositions and per-reviewer-role finding occurrences map into all thirteen counters and surface in the committed drift report.
- **SC-016**: Upstream migration classifies before mutation, salvages region bodies and imported baselines byte-identically without reminting, blocks activation on reviewer shadow, and commits a complete live-disk migration report before `init_completed: true`.
- **SC-017**: Two disjoint runs may proceed concurrently, an overlapping request is refused with both run IDs, a foreign live journal owner cannot append, and the real two-commit-chain/two-merge harness loses no marker, journal identity, Stop-telemetry row, or decision-event line. Concurrent decision events use one checked `O_APPEND` write without an append lock, remain lossless while pruning coordinates through registered writers, and never fail open.
- **SC-018**: `/forge:learn` starts only after FR-170's archive-only commit and only when the journal-derived FR-200 `journal_patterns` output has `available` true; its three separate inputs are that mechanical output, the committed archive corpus under `.forge/history/runs/`, and the current committed `.forge/history/gotchas.md` when present. It can produce only advisory candidates plus byte-preserving traceable gotcha appends, never auto-applies or commits them, never writes a control, and never blocks run close.
- **SC-019**: With phase 1 live, a commit chain driven only by CLI verbs completes end to end with every evidence record CLI-captured and candidate-bound; the hook dual-accepts chain- and marker-authorized commits with FR-090's denial literals byte-identical; a control-class chain cannot produce a commit without an operator-recorded approval naming the exact candidate; the FR-223 evals pass before any phase-1 surface ships; and disabling any single internal finalize check in memory makes its focused test fail.
- **SC-020**: An out-of-root citation cannot enter a journal through the writer tools (the append refuses with the exact FR-017 diagnostic and writes nothing); a closed journal carrying such citations archives only under explicit operator-directed FR-018 dispensation, with every excused citation, path, and reason visible in the committed archive and every non-dispensed refusal unchanged; and no-flag validation and audit behavior is byte-identical to the strict path.
- **SC-021**: A clean registered non-main worktree completes the phase-3 CLI path from atomic admission through current Gates 1–4, a bounded locked integration epoch, exact ordinary non-force push after expected-old ancestry proof, durable `pushed` truth, and non-force contained cleanup; every record binds the exact DM-014 generation, no external review/approval occurs under the common lock, and no merge commit, force operation, implicit chain selection, or `merge skip` exists.
- **SC-022**: A remote-only advance retains review/approval only through an immutable generation carry-forward with the exact equality proof and fresh integrated Gates 1–2; any candidate-HEAD rewrite, changed diff/policy, or conflict resolution instead parks outside the lock for fresh Gates 3–4 and renewed control approval naming the integrated full HEAD, while a final mechanical non-PASS leaves the remote untouched.
- **SC-023**: Rebase, push, lock, process, and cleanup crash windows recover from fsynced intent plus authoritative Git/remote observation: exact conflict paths alone may continue; standalone push accepts either the exact already-integrated no-op predicate or a crash-completed rewrite with its exact nonce/action/main-checkout/reflog predicate, while append-only operation results preserve actual failure precedence; attribution ambiguity remains observation-only without changing an actual-failure `rebase-failed` output; unknown push outcome is never blindly retried; current-head containment establishes `pushed`/`closed`, whereas older-only containment records partial `landed_head`, forbids abort/blind retry, and either admits the identity-bound fresh integrated head into history even when its OID deliberately repeats or takes the exact inactive tagged-terminal release without deleting the newer work; cleanup rechecks only current pushed truth; partial non-force cleanup is idempotently retryable without another push; every macOS/Linux entrant contends on the mandatory portable arbiter, standalone push also holds the commit lock, its nonterminal singleton interlocks commit finalize after a crash except for the exact canonical activation repair, invalid mode admits only closed recovery of an existing transaction, and its ordered inner/outer release is recoverable without replay; a proven-dead portable owner/fence is recoverable through the sole provisional reservation; an unprovably live child globally fences every merge/push; and foreign/unclassifiable merge state sets `foreign-git-state` rather than guessing.
- **SC-024**: Before activation, `forge push` is legal and `legacy-v1` preserves phase-1 behavior byte-identically; after committed `forge-verbs-v1`, raw `git commit` and raw `git push` receive the exact denials while `forge commit finalize`, `forge merge finalize`, and `forge push` remain legal, compound raw segments are still denied, malformed mode fails closed but is repairable through the exact approved canonical CLI finalize even from its standalone activation-recovery singleton, any strict-valid nonterminal transaction under invalid mode admits only non-history identity-bound recovery/death proof/owned restoration/observation/release and never new integration or push, every adjacent history-producing tuple remains interlocked, and standalone push never requires chain-produced provenance.
- **SC-025**: Every v1 corpus/eval/manifest byte remains immutable while the exact DM-016 v2 inventory pins the 41-member union, matcher, transition, recovery, push, cleanup, activated-denial, and last-line controls; Revision 7 neither deletes DM-006 nor implements the reserved phase-6 verbs, and a future deletion remains blocked unless all eleven FR-242 predicates independently PASS with binding review and explicit operator approval.

---

- **SC-026**: The committed 109-record GH#5 fixture validates under its line-107 declaration with zero issues, unchanged ten non-passing verifications, and exactly 20 warnings, while disabling the `completed` mapping or any new tolerance leg restores only its exact 4/4/1/1/8 issue partition and every at/post-declaration, `run_closed`, retirement, and FR-019 append control remains strict.
- **SC-027**: Every activated journal mutation is constructed by a typed builder and commits through one same-inode idempotent batch transaction. Readers never observe or repair a pending suffix; every enumerated crash position recovers only from base/exact-prefix/exact-complete journal and receipt-ledger truth, including a torn receipt and an already-written receipt with a retained intent; a lost-response retry returns the request-bound original receipt before allocating new values; conflicting keys or divergent bytes fail closed; and no raw JSON, replacement-inode, implicit-ID, or latest-run/task path exists.
- **SC-028**: Commit and merge chains bind one run/task tuple, carry complete typed journal batches in their consequential events through the nonrecursive source-projection/final-digest order, receipt them before advancing, and replay pending drains exactly once. The journal-outer lock order serializes all bound-chain creation/mutation with task/run terminal checks and append without a persistent close barrier or cross-chain lock set. FR-021 correlates gates/approval/landing/task order from journal fields only; proof-complete ingest produces the same ordinary seven-type outcome for already-landed chains; and every missing binding/outbox/ingest control has a focused disable-in-memory test that fails.
- **SC-029**: Every new run archive deterministically embeds exact fenced state and exact event bytes through 2 MiB, uses a visible digest-only `UNEMBEDDED` block above that threshold, refuses beyond 16 MiB, renders only resolved structured bindings, and can commit only when the absent direct-child candidate equals a fresh rerender with no other staged path. The archive-only chain is self-carriage-exempt, not post-close journaled, and every existing DM-008 archive remains byte-identical.
- **SC-030**: The absent phase-0 archive can be created only from its immutable closed journal under one explicit operator decision naming the recovered full HEAD, with the two exact legacy labels and all ordinary rerender/path/index checks; no manifest artifact exists. The additive v3 corpus pins the complete 53-member `forge-cli/2` union while every v1/v2 row and artifact remains unchanged.

2. **Level B validate** — FR-020..FR-025, FR-014. Outcome: `--gates` implemented and tested; replay extended; contract doc updated.

Outcome: scoped advisory mutation with bounded timeouts/malformed-row evidence, Python-only blocking assertion sensing with narrow waivers, seeded advisory heuristics, and committed-policy invariant checks operate at commit, merge, hook, and drift surfaces.
